## Document 001: -335亿美元！亚马逊的这个数字，“吓蒙”了市场

Source PDF: all report pdf/-335亿美元！亚马逊的这个数字，“吓蒙”了市场.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

亚⻢逊三季度⾃由现⾦流骤降⾄ -335 亿美元，较市场预期 -77 亿美元超出四倍，背后是单季
773 亿资本开⽀与 481 亿新增债务的双重冲击。 AWS 增速虽创五年新⾼达 39.3% ，却仍低于
买⽅预期，利润率反向下滑。四季度运营利润指引 310 亿，全⾯低于华尔街及买⽅预期。核
⼼症结在于： AWS 每赚 1 美元，背后捆绑近 4 美元新增⽀出。
摘要：
亚⻢逊三季度财报⽤⼀个极其惨烈的数字回答了华尔街全年的疑问：单季⾃由现⾦流消耗⾼达
335 亿美元。尽管 AWS 云业务创下近五年最快增速，但失控的资本⽀出（ Capex ）、疲软的四季
度指引以及暴增的债务，彻底击碎了买⽅的乐观预期。
亚⻢逊最新财报显⽰，三季度⾃由现⾦流录得 -335 亿美元，⽽彭博市场⼀致预期仅为 -77 亿美
元，实际结果超出预期四倍以上。 单季资本开⽀飙升⾄ 773 亿美元，环⽐增⻓ 43% ；为填补资
⾦缺⼝，公司在该季度新增 481 亿美元⻓期债务。滚动 12 个⽉⾃由现⾦流从上季度的 -76 亿美元

急剧恶化⾄ -424 亿美元。
与此同时，四季度业绩指引令市场失望。营收指引中值为 2435 亿美元，略低于市场⼀致预期的
2442 亿美元；运营利润指引中值 310 亿美元，不仅低于华尔街预期的 330 亿美元，更全⾯低于买
⽅层⾯ 350 亿美元的 " ⼼理关⼝ " 。 ⾼盛在盘后快评中指出，多项核⼼指标相对于市场预期呈
现 " 中性偏弱 " 结果，预计股价将出现初步负⾯反应。
财报公布后，亚⻢逊盘后股价⼀度暴跌 12.5% ⾄ 223 美元附近（最低触及约 214 美元），远超
7.5% 的隐含波动率，抹平了⼆季度以来的全部涨幅，回落⾄今年 4 ⽉初⽔平。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

对于投资者⽽⾔，核⼼逻辑已经发⽣根本性转变：亚⻢逊的股票之所以 “ 涨不动 ” ，是因为 AWS
业务每带来 1 美元的潜在收益，背后都捆绑着远超 1 美元（甚⾄接近 4 美元）的新增⽀出。华尔街
的预期游戏已经失效，现在市场必须直⾯⾼昂的账单。
那个关键数字：单季 -335 亿美元
如果说有⼀个数字概括了亚⻢逊当前的处境，那就是-335 亿美元—— 这是公司三季度单季⾃由
现⾦流。
这⼀数字的背后逻辑极为直⽩：单季经营性现⾦流约 424 亿美元，但净资本开⽀⾼达 759 亿美
元，两者之差即为现⾦⿊洞。⽽就在⼀年前，亚⻢逊的⾃由现⾦流还处于 529 亿美元的历史峰
值。
资本开⽀的膨胀速度同样触⽬惊⼼。三季度⽑资本开⽀达 773 亿美元，环⽐⼆季度的 542 亿美元
增⻓ 43% 。亚⻢逊同时确认，预计 2026 年全年资本开⽀约 2200 亿美元的计划不变。按前三季度
已累计净⽀出约 1720 亿美元计算，四季度资本开⽀将约为 480 亿美元，较刚刚完成的三季度骤降
约 37% 。
这⼀测算令投资者⽣疑：要么亚⻢逊将在四季度⼤幅收紧⽀出，与 CEO Andy Jassy 所描述的 " 需

求持续超过供给 " 形成⽭盾；要么三季度存在⼤量⽀出的提前确认，使得原有指引本⾝已失效。
⽆论哪种情形，市场均难以认同 2027 年资本开⽀将低于 2026 年 —— 此前买⽅预期明年资本开⽀
在 3200 亿⾄ 3750 亿美元之间，⽽亚⻢逊在本次财报中对 2027 年的数字只字未提。
谁来买单？债务飙升，资产负债表急剧恶化

### Page 3

当运营现⾦⽆法覆盖⽀出时，钱只能从别处来。亚⻢逊的资产负债表给出了答案：疯狂发债。
为了⽀撑庞⼤的开⽀，亚⻢逊在三季度筹集了 481 亿美元的新增⻓期债务。今年前九个⽉，公司
新增⻓期借款⾼达 1151 亿美元，导致⻓期债务⼏乎翻了三倍，从 2025 年底的 656 亿美元飙升⾄ 9
⽉底的 1731 亿美元。
与此同时，公司的财务健康度正在全⾯下滑：
现⾦储备从 868 亿美元骤降⾄ 558 亿美元。
利息⽀出同⽐翻了三倍多，从去年同期的 5 亿美元飙升⾄ 17 亿美元。
“ 其他⻓期负债 ” 也⼏乎翻了三倍，从 360 亿美元增⾄ 999 亿美元。
值得注意的是，上述数据还不包括亚⻢逊⾸次发⾏的英镑计价债券，以及⽤于购买英伟达芯⽚
的 80 亿美元特殊⽬的载体安排。
正如市场所观察到的逻辑链条： “债务变为资本开⽀，资本开⽀变为营收，营收变为每股收益。 ”
亚⻢逊本季度在规模上完整展⽰了这条链条的第⼀个环节。
AWS 与核⼼业务：五年最佳增速仍难填买⽅胃⼝
尽管 AWS 云业务表现强劲，营收增⻓ 39.3% ⾄ 460 亿美元，创下近五年最快增速，且 CEO Jassy

给出了 2027 和 2028 年 AWS 增⻓ “ 超过 40%” 的乐观预期。但这对挑剔的买⽅来说还不够：39.3%
的增速虽然击败了华尔街约 37% 的预期，但低于买⽅真正期待的 42% 。
更糟糕的是利润率⾛向了错误的⽅向。AWS 营业利润率从⼆季度的 39.4% 下滑⾄ 38.7%，未能
达到买⽅预期的 “40% 出头 ” 。
其他业务⽅⾯，零售表现平稳（北美销售额增⻓ 6% ，国际增⻓ 8% ，剔除汇率影响）。但⼴告业
务增速放缓⾄ 12%（剔除汇率影响），公司将其归咎于 Prime Day 的 “ 时间异常 ” ，并承诺四季度将
反弹⾄⾼⼗位数增⻓。
此外，每股收益（ EPS ）录得 1.89 美元，不及预期的 1.97 美元。这主要是因为去年三季度包含了
Anthropic 股份带来的 95 亿美元税前收益，⽽今年⾮运营项⽬反⽽造成了 12 亿美元的拖累（包含

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
翻倍的利息账单）。唯⼀符合预期的是营业利润，达到创纪录的 281 亿美元（利润率 13.9% ），精
准命中买⽅传⾔的 280 亿美元。
疲软的四季度指引：全⾯不及预期
四季度业绩指引是此次财报的另⼀个失望来源。
营收指引区间为 2400 亿⾄ 2470 亿美元，中值 2435 亿美元，略低于市场⼀致预期的 2442 亿美
元。运营利润指引为 285 亿⾄ 335 亿美元，中值 310 亿美元，不仅低于华尔街预期的 330 亿美元，
也低于买⽅⼼理关⼝的 300 亿⾄ 350 亿美元区间的两端，缺⼝超过 15 亿美元。
据摩根⼤通分析师 Mark Schilsky 此前预测，⼀旦亚⻢逊内部 AI 助⼿ Leo 正式上线、相关成本开始
资本化，运营利润将获得约 10 亿美元的额外提振 —— 即便如此，实际指引仍未能达到预期。

⼴告业务是指引中唯⼀的亮点：三季度⼴告增速放缓⾄ 12% （剔除汇率影响），公司将此归因于
Prime Day 的 “ 时间性异常 ” ，并预计四季度将反弹⾄ ” ⾼⼗字头 ” 增速区间。不过，随着亚⻢逊屏
蔽 Meta Muse 等 AI 购物代理、市场担忧 AI 代理购物将侵蚀赞助商品⼴告收⼊，⼴告业务的⻓期前
景仍存争议。
为什么这只股票 “ 永远涨不动 ” ？
摩根⼤通分析师 Mark Schilsky 在财报前已对亚⻢逊的困境作出精准诊断：
据⾼盛和瑞银的评估，亚⻢逊财报前是科技股中拥挤度最⾼的多头头⼨之⼀，⾼盛 TMT 桌拥挤
度评分 8/10 ，瑞银情绪量表 9/10 。⾼度拥挤的多头持仓意味着，⼀旦现实未能匹配预期，⽌损卖
压将成倍放⼤。
三季度财报的结构性⽭盾清晰可⻅： AWS 加速兑现，但每⼀美元的云业务收⼊，背后跟随着远
超⼀美元的新增⽀出。本季度，这⼀⽐例接近四⽐⼀。  只要这⼀模式未能扭转，股价上涨的逻
辑便始终⾯临来⾃资产负债表的压制。
这张账单是：单季 773 亿美元的资本⽀出、 335 亿美元的现⾦消耗，以及 481 亿美元的新增债

务。亚⻢逊的股票之所以表现挣扎，是因为 AWS 带来的每⼀美元上⾏空间，都捆绑着超过⼀美
元的新增⽀出 —— ⽽最新财报显⽰，这个⽐例接近⼀⽐四。
市场终于意识到，⾼昂的基础设施投⼊正在⽆情地吞噬股东的现⾦流。
“AMZN 是今年最让主流投资者沮丧的股票之⼀ …… 华尔街对 2027 年 AWS 的预测低得可笑 …… 正
因如此，投资者都把它视为容易超预期的故事，但所有⼈都知道这⼀点，这恰恰是它涨不动的核
⼼原因。 ”

## Document 110: 261027_ubs_faraday

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_ubs_faraday.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Faraday Technology
New opportunities in advanced nodes
Q326 beat on turnkey strength and non-op gains; Q426 outlook positive
Q326 EPS of NT$1.07 came in above UBS/Street expectations mainly on  non-op gains.
Q326 sales came in at NT$3.66 bn (+11% QoQ), ahead of its original guidance but in
line with its comment  during Taiwan Summit with turnkey sales up 20% QoQ given
better support from OSAT and foundries, despite NRE declining 31% QoQ. Q326 GM

decreased 2.7ppt QoQ to 42.5% on higher turnkey mix, while  OPEX grew 9% QoQ  on
salary adjustment and higher bonus accrual. Non-op gain of NT$266mn mainly includes
valuation gains on an investee company. For Q426F, it guided sales  flat  QoQ remaining
at high levels with IP and NRE up QoQ, and turnkey down QoQ. GM is guided back to
~45%, reflecting normalized ASIC economics; OPEX should decline single digit QoQ.
2027 growth outlook supported by AI, advanced packaging and 12nm node

Management guided for double-digit sales growth in 2027, with all three businesses, IP ,
NRE and turnkey, expected to grow, driven by three key catalysts: AI, advanced
packaging and new UMC 12nm project contribution. For IP , growth should be
supported by higher royalties on foundry price hikes/customer capacity expansion and
initial  contribution from 12nm node; NRE should benefit from 2nm/4nm projects
entering peak recognition, with 3-4 ASIC tape-outs targeted in H127; while MP should

see growth in both MCU and ASIC as new projects gradually ramp. AI-related mix was at
8-9% of Q326 sales, and is expected to increase throughout 2027 alongside
accelerating advanced-packaging shipments. We expect 2027E sales to grow 37% YoY.
2nm design win opens new advanced-node and product opportunities
Faraday secured a 2nm GAA special purpose  ASIC project worth several tens of millions
of US dollars in NRE, which management described as its largest-ever design win and

most advanced project to date. The project spans front-end design and IP integration,
back-end design, customized 3D DRAM and advanced packaging, and marks Faraday's
first customized 3D DRAM and hybrid-bonding project. It expects to recognize its first
NRE milestone  in H127, followed by MP targeted within 1-2 years. Beyond 2nm, Faraday
is expanding into new applications including OCS, CXL and Si-cap, leveraging its
capabilities in advanced packaging, interposer design and ASIC integration. We believe

successful execution by Faraday and its foundry partner could strengthen Faraday's
positioning in advanced-node projects and create follow-on design-win opportunities.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$200, stay Neutral
We raise    2026E EPS (reported) from NT$3.43 to NT$3.68 on better Q326, but lower
2027/28E EPS by 4%/1% to factor in softer CE demand and higher OPEX. We raise our
PT to NT$200 from NT$190 using 24x 2027E PE (vs prior 22x). We raise our multiple as

the recent 2nm project win should boost investor confidence, but stay Neutral as the
advanced nodes (4nm/2nm) turnkey revenue recognition depends on the foundry
partner's execution. We await clearer evidence before revisiting our assumptions.

Forecast returns

Forecast stock return 5.4%

Company Description
Faraday was founded in 1993, spun off from UMC's IP and semiconductor design service
department as the first IC design service company in Asia. It is an IC design and production
service provider that initially focused on specialty applications on mature nodes by partnering

with UMC and gradually upgraded to advanced nodes through working with Samsung. The
company had its IPO on the Taiwan Securities Exchange in 1999.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
We value Faraday using PE methodology.
Downside risks include slower-than-expected advanced nodes migration, further delay of
advanced packaging ramp, and growing competition in specialty projects from China IC
design service companies.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

M
Update
Accton Technology Corporation  | Asia Pacific
Read-across from Celestica's
3Q26 Results
We see positive implications for Accton given demand strength
(1.6T migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up
opportunities) of ethernet  switches in future.
Celestica (CLS.N, not covered) 3Q26 results:
• 3Q26 revenue of US$5,830mn (+24% QoQ/+83% YoY), non-GAAP operating
margin of 8.5%,  a record high, and non-GAAP EPS of US$3.30 (+30% QoQ/

+109% YoY).
° Higher than guidance and 7%/13% above consensus for revenue/earnings,
respectively.
° Drivers: Stronger CCS revenue (84% of the mix in 3Q26), including
networking and compute/storage growth of 68% and 242% YoY,
respectively, vs. guidance 60% and 190% YoY.
• 4Q26 revenue guided at US$6,275-6,575mn (+10% QoQ/+76% YoY at the
midpoint) vs. consensus at US$6,396mn, management raising 2026 target to
US$21bn for revenue (+69% YoY vs. prior US$20.5bn) and US$11.55 for non-

GAAP EPS  (+91% YoY vs. prior US$11.3).
° Driven by hyperscaler-led 800G demand and ramping 1.6T programs,
along with continued AI compute and storage demand.
• 2027 revenue guided at US$40bn (+90% YoY), non-GAAP OPM at 9.0%
(+0.6ppt YoY) and non-GAAP EPS at US$21.5 (+86% YoY)
° It expects CCS to grow 110% (1.6T/800G on networking and ASIC ramp
on compute/storage) and  ATS at 10%, with revenue skewed to  2H on
multiple large ramps and momentum likely sustaining into  2028.

• Pluggable should remain the mainstream interconnect solution, while CPO
might pick up more meaningfully from 3.2T, per company.
• CLS expects ethernet scale up could represent new opportunities in future.
Our view:
• While the customer profiles are different, we still see positive implications
from Celestica's comments for Accton regarding demand strength (1.6T
migration amid sustaining 800G) and breadth (scale up opportunities) of
ethernet  switches.

• We stay constructive on Accton, and believe   it will continue to benefit from
the secular growth in network switches amid increasing data traffic, spec
upgrades, and a wider customer base as well as the AI accelerator modules
on the initiatives for compute diversity.

More Takeaways
Further comments from Celestica:
• Demand visibility is lengthening and it is now having conversations with customers
for 2029/2030 projects
• Still seeing lots of pluggables during 1.6T, while it expects CPO to play out more
prominently in 3.2T.
• It has won a new hyperscaler program to co-design and manufacture a 1.6T rack-
scale CPO networking solution, with production scheduled to ramp in late 2027,

per management.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Accton Technology Corporation (2345.TW)
Base case, P/E, which we believe better captures the company's earnings growth momentum.
We assign a target P/E of 30x to our 2027 EPS estimate, which we view as justified by an
operating profit CAGR of 48% for 2025-28, based on our estimates. This implies a PEG of
around 0.6, vs. its average of 0.7 over 2019-25, when the stock was trading at a P/E of 22x for
an operating profit CAGR of 32%.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger data center capex
n Faster network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand losing share in AI networks
n Stronger custom AI accelerator growth
n Stronger ODM direct business model adoption
Risks to Downside
n Weaker data center capex
n Slower network switch spec migration
n InfiniBand taking more share in AI networks
n Softer custom AI accelerator growth
n Softer ODM direct business model adoption

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 201: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로삼성전자(005930) 긴 호흡으로긴 호흡으로 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

(005930) 
삼성전자 
2026.09.29

반도체 손인준 
6154 / sij@eugenefn.com

긴 호흡으로 
 투자의견 STRONG BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 560,000원(유지) 
 현재주가 270,000원(9/28)

 삼성전자의 3Q26 영업이익이 103.6조원(+16%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 메모리 가격 등 전반적인 반도체 업황에 대한 기존 가정은 대체로 
유지했으나, 최근 원화 강세에 따라 원/달러 환율 가정을 1,440원에서 
1,400원으로 낮추고 MX 사업부의 적자 확대를 반영해 실적 추정치를 하
향 조정했다. 또한 2Q26 재고가 3Q26 매출로 인식되는 과정에서 발생하
는 추가 성과급 충당금 부담도 반영했다.  
 DS 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 104.0조원(+17%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. 서버 DRAM과 eSSD 중심의 가격 인상이 이어지며, DRAM과 
NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +17%, +18%QoQ 상승할 것으로 추정한
다. 1cnm 수율 개선과 HBM4의 빠른 램프업도 실적 개선에 기여할 전망이
다. Foundry/LSI는 HBM4 베이스다이 생산 확대, Groq 3 LPU 양산 및 선
단 공정 가격 인상에 힘입어 하반기 매출 성장이 예상된다. 다만 성과급 충
당금과 감가상각비 부담으로 영업적자 축소 폭은 제한적일 것으로 보인다. 
 DX 부문의 3Q26 영업이익은 -1.8조원(적자지속qoq)을 기록할 것으로 추
정한다. 삼성전자는 메모리 가격 급등에도 제품 가격 인상을 제한하며 시장 
점유율 확대에 주력하고 있다. 이러한 가격 정책과 갤럭시 Z 폴드8 등 신제
품 흥행에 힘입어 판매량은 당초 예상치를 상회할 것으로 보인다. 다만 원
가 상승분의 전가가 제한되면서 영업적자 폭은 크게 확대될 전망이다.  
 삼성전자의 4Q26 영업이익은 114.9조원(+11%qoq)을 기록할 것으로 전
망한다. DRAM과 NAND의 Blended ASP는 각각 +11%, +12%QoQ 상승
하며 견조한 흐름을 이어갈 것으로 보인다. 환율 가정 조정에 따라 2026년
과 2027년 영업이익 추정치를 각각 365.2조원(+735%yoy), 595.8조원
(+63%YoY)으로 낮췄다. 4Q26~1Q27 범용 DRAM·NAND 및 2027년 
HBM 가격 협상 환경은 공급업체에 유리하게 전개되고 있어, 향후 가격 가
정의 추가 상향 가능성이 높다고 판단한다.  
 극심한 DRAM 공급 부족으로 인한 HBM 적층 단수 하향 조정(base die 수
요 증가), Memory Wall에 대응하기 위한 3D Stacked DRAM·zHBM 도입 
논의 등은 파운드리의 기술적 가치를 부각시키는 요인이다. 파운드리의 수
익성 회복 속도는 완만할 전망이지만, 메모리와 파운드리를 연계한 턴키 비
즈니스의 시너지는 AI 반도체 시장 내 경쟁력을 높이고, 메모리 시장 점유
율 확대에도 기여할 것으로 보인다. 적극적인 메모리 가격 인상 정책으로 
업계 최고 수준의 Blended ASP도 장기간 유지할 것으로 예상한다. 
 이제 메모리 업종 주가는 업황의 지속 가능성을 확인하고 꾸준한 주주환원
을 반영하며, 중장기적으로 점진적인 상승세를 이어갈 것으로 판단한다. 삼
성전자는 메모리·파운드리를 아우르는 턴키 역량과 가격 정책에 기반한 실
적 차별화를 통해 피어 그룹 내 경쟁력을 높여갈 것으로 보인다.

Compliance Notice 
당사는 자료 작성일 기준으로 지난 3 개월 간 해당종목에 대해서 유가증권 발행에 참여한 적이 없습니다 
당사는 본 자료 발간일을 기준으로 해당종목의 주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다  
당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

투자기간 및 투자등급/투자의견 비율

주가 추이

3분기 영업 이 익 은 지상방산 매출 감소와 일 회성 비용 등의 영 향 으로 
기대치를 대폭 하회했습니다. 4분기 실적 회복 과 중 장기 실적 성장 대
비 저평가된 밸 류 에이 션 에 주목합 니 다. 
3Q26 Review: 영업이익 0.5조원으로 컨센서스 대폭 하회 
3분기 매출액은 6.1조원으로 전년대비 6.0% 감소했다. 항공우주 부문
과 연결자회사들 성장에도 불구하고 지상방산 매출이 전년대비 약 0.9
조원 감소한 영향이다. 지상방산 내수와 수출 매출은 각각 0.7조원, 0.6
조원으로 전년대비 28.6%, 50.4% 감소했다. 내수는 대전 사업장 생산 
중단, 수출은 4분기 납기 집중에 기인한다. 항공우주 매출은 군수사업 
물량 증가로 전년대비 9.3% 증가한 0.7조원을 기록했다. 연결 자회사
인 한화오션과 한화시스템 매출은 각각 3.4조원, 0.8조원으로 전년대비 
12.9%, 30.1% 증가했다. 영업이익은 0.5조원으로 전년대비 49.2% 감
소했으며 영업이익률은 7.4%로 6.3%P 하락했다. 한화오션과 한화시스
템의 이익 증가에도 지상방산 이익 감소와 필리조선소 적자 반영이 영
향을 미쳤다. 지상방산은 일회성 비용 약 460억원, 원화 강세, 매출 감
소에 따른 고정비 부담 증가가 복합적으로 작용했다. 세전이익은 한화
오션의 외화환산손실 영향으로 -0.2조원을 기록하며 적자전환했다.  
실적 회복과 저평가 매력에 주목할 시점 
4분기 영업이익은 1.7조원으로 추정하며 시장 기대치를 상회할 전망이
다. 지상방산 수출 인도 물량이 집중되는 영향이다. 2027년 예상 실적 
기준 P/E는 12.5배로 경쟁사 Rheinmetall(22.5배) 대비 낮다. 약 35조
원의 방산 수주잔고를 기반으로 최소 2029년까지 실적 성장이 예상되
는 만큼 저평가 매력이 높다. 신규 수주 시점과 실적에 대한 우려가 상
존하지만 현재 밸류에이션에는 상당 부분 반영된 것으로 판단한다. 미
국 중간선거 등 국방예산 관련 정책 불확실성과 환율 부담이 완화되면 
투자자들의 관심은 4분기 실적과 2027년 신규 수주로 이동할 전망이
다. 연말 주요국의 국방예산 확정은 신규 수주 시점에 대한 불확실성을 
낮추는 계기가 될 수 있다.  
투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 1,300,000원 유지 
글로벌 재무장 수요 확대에 따른 동사의 중장기 성장 전망은 여전히 
유효하다. 투자의견 BUY와 목표주가 1,300,000원을 유지한다.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 30 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (채운샘) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
한화에어로스페이스는 당사와 ‘독점규제 및 공정거래에 관한 법률’ 에 따른 계열회사 관계에 있는 법인입니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 6

한화에어로스페이스 (012450) [한화리서치]

6 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일)

### Page 1

삼성바이오로직스 (207940/KS) 
3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 
2026-10-06

제약/바이오. 이선경 / seonkyoung.lee@sks.co.kr / 3773-9089

매수(유지) 
목표주가: 2,000,000 원(유지) 
현재주가: 1,354,000 원 
상승여력: 47.7%

Signal: 파업과 환율 부담에도 컨센 부합 전망  
Key: 5 공장 매출 본격화, 미국 공장 매출 기여에 따른 성장 기대   
Step: 단기 불확실성보다는 중장기적 성장 방향성에 주목

3Q26 Pre: 파업과 환율 부담에도 성장지속, 컨센 부합 전망   
26년 3분기 연결기준 매출액은 1조 3,741억원(+9.3%YoY, +4.0%QoQ), 영업이
익은 6,098억원(-3.7%YoY, +4.0%QoQ, OPM 44.4%)으로 컨센서스 매출액 1
조 3,450억원과 영업이익 6,063억원에 부합하는 실적을 전망한다.  
5월 파업 영향으로 약 20개 배치 생산분(약 1,500억원)에 대한 원가 손실이 인식되
고 3분기 평균 환율이 전분기 대비 약 5% 하락했음에도 불구하고, 3분기부터 매출로 
인식되는 5공장 PPQ 배치 및 미국 공장의 매출 기여로 지속적인 성장이 예상된다. 약 
1,500억원 규모의 생산 차질분은 고객사와 일정 조율을 통해 출하될 예정으로 이에 
따른 4분기 매출 성장을 기대해 볼 수 있다.   
신규 수주 회복과 모달리티 확장에 주목 
시장에서 우려했던 신규 수주는 관세 이슈로 고객사의 컨택이 감소하면서 올해 저조했
으나 4 월 Section 232 결과 발표 이후 논의가 회복 단계에 있어 연내 추가 신규 수주 
가능성은 열려 있다.  
항체 시장의 경쟁심화와 차세대 모달리티 확장에 대응하기 위해 결정한 폴리펩타이드
의 인수는 연내 마무리될 예정이다. 폴리펩타이드의 2028 년 목표 EBITDA 마진은 
25%로 동사 대비 낮은 상황이나, 펩타이드 기반 CDMO 시장 성장에 따른 마진 개선
도 가능하다. 현재 임단협, 환율, 미국 공장 및 폴리펩타이드 인수에 따른 회계 영향 등 
불확실성이 큰 상황이나, 수주 회복과 5 공장 매출 인식 본격화, 미국 공장 및 멀티 모
달리티 확장 등 중장기 성장 방향성에 주목할 필요가 있다.

## Document 226: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다HD현대중공업(329180) 발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-17
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

00

HD 현대중공업 (329180/KS) 
발전 엔진으로 또 성장한다 
기다렸던 4 행정 중속 엔진 캐파 증설 단행 
HD현대중공업의 4행정 엔진 캐파는 기존 3.0GW에서 증설이 완료되는 '28년부터 
램프업을 거쳐 '30년 7.2GW까지 확대될 계획이다. ①[육상 발전용]은 신공장(울산 
온산) 3GW 와 HD 현대엔진(전남 영암) 1GW 를 합쳐 4.0GW, ②[선박용]은 
2.3GW에서 3.2GW로 확대된다. HD현대엔진 캐파는 기존 0.7GW에서 1GW까
지 확대되며, 해당 라인은 신공장이 가동되기 전 ‘27~’28 년 구간에서의 선박&육상 엔
진 모두 대응 가능하다. 신공장 캐파는 총 3.0GW로, ‘28년 약 30%의 가동률로 시
작해 램프업을 거쳐 ‘30 년부터 풀캐파로 20MW급 모델을 생산할 예정이다. 4 행정 
중속엔진에 대한 수요 가시성은 높다. 동사가 공유했던 육상 발전 엔진 인콰이어리 규
모는 기존 약 3~9GW였으나, 현재는 이를 크게 상회하는 수준으로 소통하고 있다.

최근 부유식 데이터센터(FDC)에 대한 하이퍼스케일러들의 인콰이어리 또한 확대되고 
있다. HD 한국조선해양은 최근 미국 선급(ABS)로부터 100MW급 규모의 부유식 발
전 설비(BMPP)에 대한 기본 인증(AiP)를 획득했으며, 동사는 FDC 신조 사업과 더불
어 발전원 4 행정 엔진을 파워십 및 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)에 납품하며 그룹사들과 
패키지 사업으로 진행할 예정이다. 가시화된 수요를 기반으로 엔진기계사업부의 구조적 
성장 보여줄 것으로 전망하며, 이에 따른 리레이팅을 예상한다.

투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원 유지 
HD 현대중공업에 대한 투자의견 ‘매수’ 및 목표주가 기존 97 만원을 유지한다. 동사는 
올해 상선수주 목표 이미 초과달성하며 안정적인 펀더멘털 유지 중이다. 하반기 남은 
기간은 LNGC, VLGC/VLAC 중심의 선별 수주 전략을 통해 ‘28 년 이후의 상선 부
문 수익성 유지 충분히 가능할 것으로 예상된다. 글로벌 지정학적 리스크 확대에 따른 
해군 현대화 사이클 기조에 맞추어 미 해군은 동맹국의 호위함 구매를 고려하고 있으
며, MASGA를 기반으로 중장기적으로 미국 전투함을 건조하기 위해 HD현대의 미국 
진출 가능성 높다는 판단이다. 또한 미국을 제외한 글로벌 함정(페루, 필리핀, 말레이시
아, 칠레, 사우디, 이집트 등) 수주 파이프라인도 다수 보유하고 있어 함정 모멘텀에 따
른 리레이팅 충분히 가능할 전망이다. 특수선+4 행정 엔진+FDC+SMR 등 조선소에 
붙일 수 있는 모든 추가 성장동력을 보유한 유일한 종목이기에 조선업 최선호주(Top-
Pick)로 제시한다. 최근 불거지고 있는 노조 파업 문제 해소될 시 저점 매수 기회라는 
판단이다.

2026-09-17

조선/방산. 한승한, R.A 고서영 / shane.han@sks.co.kr / 3773-9992

TAKING STOCK

The Reserve Bank of India (RBI) has adopted a 
multi-layered defence strategy to support the 
rupee this year.

This has entailed a combination of outright 
intervention in spot/ forwards, regulatory 
action, liquidity steps, administrative measures 
and moves to curb import -related dollar 
demand.

The rupee has appreciated by 1.8% against the 
dollar in the past five days since recent 
measures took effect, paring YTD depreciation 
to 5.5% . High oil prices, weakness in capital 
inflows, a firm dollar , tariffs/ geopolitical 
worries, energy , and risk -off sentiments have 
kept the INR under pressure since last year.

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026

ECHOES OF 2013 WITH FEW 
DIFFERENCES

Comparisons with 2013 are apt, though 
the backdrop differs.  As for the 
catalysts, markets reacted to the US 
Fed’s taper announcement in 2013, 
which pushed US yields higher  and led 
global investors to reduce exposure to 
vulnerable EM economies. As a result, 
capital inflows weakened, exposing 
funding mismatch es for countries, 
including India, that were running wide 
current account deficits.

In 2026, markets face the spectre of 
elevated oil prices, geopolitical 
uncertainty, global competition for 
capital, a firm dollar ( DXY appreciated 
+3.8% YTD) , and US inflation/ deficits 
which have settled at levels much higher 
than pre -pandemic years. This has 
pushed up UST yields, and the US Fed 
kicked off its rate-hike cycle in Sep26.

Against this backdrop, the RBI is 
pursuing the same objective , i.e., to 
ensure rupee stability, but the nature of 
the battle differs. The defence strategy 
has shifted from ‘crisis management’ 
mode in 2013 , due to weaker domestic 
macroeconomic balances (a wide current 
account gap, high inflation, a lower 
reserve buffer, and wide fiscal deficits) , 
to a ‘terms-of-trade shock and impact of 
a firm dollar’ in 2026.

Flashpoints are  nonetheless similar, 
with reliance on portfolio financing , 
leaving the external account vulnerable 
to shifts in global risk sentiment. 
Portfolio outflows have emerged as the 
primary transmission channel through 
which external shocks have translated 
into INR depreciation pressure , 
compounded by high energy prices and a 
firm dollar.

FX PLAYBOOK

While the response framework is similar 
between 2013 and this year, the broad 
sequence and nature of macroprudential 
measures differ. This year’s multi-
layered rupee defence toolkit has 
included:

-Direct FX defence  by way of 
intervention in the spot and forwards 
market. As of Aug26, the net forward 
dollar liabilities ballooned to $200bn, 
rising by ~$63bn vs . the month before . 
Recent sell/buy FX swaps are expected 
to lower this stock in the coming months.

-Attracting dollar inflows  by way of 
special swap facilities for FCNR deposits, 
and offshore borrowings , apart from 
liberalising taxes around debt inflows,

### Page 3

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

amongst others. We discussed this here, 
here, here and here.

Taking a proactive stance, a portion of 
existing FX reserves could be earmarked 
against forward liabilities (from maturing 
FCNR deposits) , helping to mitigate 
concerns that deposit maturities or debt 
repayments could trigger a sharp
increase in dollar ⁠demand down the line.

-Domestic liquidity management. Bonds, 
to some degree, are paying the price for 
the authorities' efforts to defend the 
rupee against the additional headwind of 
weak global conditions.  INR liquidity 
surged on the back of the swap windows, 
prompting the RBI to undertake open 
market operations, VRRR auctions, sell -
buy swaps, among others. While these 
have been effective in absorbing 
balances, bond yields have drifted higher. 
10Y yields are at three-year highs and are 
expected to climb further  (DBSf: 7.40 -
7.50% by year-end).

-Moderate FX demand and speculation . 
Earlier in the year, the RBI had 
introduced change s to the banks’ net 
open position framework, and thereafter 
partially rolled back a few curbs. 
Prudential measures on FX exposures 
and hedging -related measures were 
announced in Oct26, which include d the 
introduction of a daily cash reserve with 
the RBI equal to 20% of the rupee 
equivalent of the notional value for INR -
linked derivative contracts exceeding 
$2mn used to hedge current account 
transactions, referred to as the Foreign 
Exchange Risk Reserve (FERR) . 
Subsequent clarifications provided 
operational details on where the FERR 
would be maintained and that it would be 
subject to audits.

Since 12 Oct, the RBI has been providing 
dollars via designated banks to the three 
state-owned oil -marketing companies 
(Indian Oil, BPCL, and HPCL) to meet the 
their daily dollar requirements, thereby 
diverting related spot-market demand.

On hedging-related matters, the limit for 
undertaking foreign exchange derivative 
transactions without proving an 
underlying commercial exposure was 
cut to $5mn from $100mn across all ADs 
and exchange -traded currency 
derivatives. Restrictions on Rebooking , 
i.e., a uthorized dealers are prohibited 
from allowing customers to rebook 
cancelled rupee -linked derivative 
contracts (though rollovers upon 
maturity remain permitted). Lastly, 
banks must obtain written confirmation 
from users that the same underlying 
exposure has not been hedged with 
another dealer.  Data from CCI , cited by

### Page 4

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Bloomberg, pointed to importers’ 
forward dollar purchases rising 44% on 
the year to a monthly average of $64bn.

-Current account management . Efforts 
to contain structural FX outflows by way 
of higher duties and taxes on gold 
imports discourage non -essential 
purchases and production incentives to 
improve export competitiveness.

IMPACT

To assess whether the playbook has 
worked, we observe:

a) Rupee has enjoyed a brief respite. 
Despite oil prices at $105/bbl at the time 
of writing and elevated global yields, 
USDINR fell from near 96.80 to wards 
95.00, owing to measures announced in 
Oct26 following the central bank’s 25bp 
hike. Beyond temporary relief, global 
catalysts are likely to be back to the fore 
and weigh on the currency (see chart);

b) Sharp inflows under the swap 
facilities pushed the foreign reserves 
stock to a record high, providing 
policymakers sufficient buffer to 
navigate future shocks from a position of 
strength. A strong interventionist 
response has since led to a softening in 
the overall stock (see chart).

c) Macroprudential measures have led to 
distortions in other parts of the market . 
For instance, strong swap -related 
inflows had inorganically pushed 
onshore INR liquidity up sharply, 
necessitating absorption measures. 
Similarly, NOP restrictions and the more 
recent tighter requirements on hedging 
activity and counterparty transactions  
had some after -effects. Forward points 
have surged this week , with financial 
institutions pointing to relative illiquidity 
in the currency forwards market  on 
volumes and activity basis, and fewer 
avenues to deal with dollar supply.

### Page 5

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

d) Swap-related windows are a timely 
support for the country’s balance of 
payments position. From expectations of 
a third consecutive year of BOP deficit in 
FY27, the math has now swung to a 
potential surplus, even as current 
account worries remain. With energy 
prices settling north of $100/bbl, 
keeping the monthly trade deficit near 
$30bn, our FY27 current account deficit 
forecast of 1.1% of GDP faces  upside 
risks of around 0.3-0.5% of GDP. We note 
the s harp jump in ECB outflows in S ep, 
though see that as a temporary overshot 
of leverage-FCNR activity.

WHAT LIES AHEAD

Authorities are likely not done yet and 
remain ready to take further action, as 
required. RBI Governor Malhotra 
reiterated the authorities’ serious intent 
of stabilising the currency in a recent 
interview (Moneycontrol, 13 Oct), 
emphasising that “The RBI is determined 
to do whatever is necessary to ensure 
orderly movement in the foreign 
exchange market,”. He also highlighted 
that recent steps were not tantamount to 
capital control measures that were 
aimed at ensuring that forex markets 
serve genuine hedging needs rather than 
speculative bets.  Market participants 
opine that FX volumes and interest are 
likely to be subdued in a few pockets on 
the back of the recent measures, 
consequently stabilizing the rupee.

Unlike in 2013, when policy tightening 
was a part of the initial response, the 
2026 strategy has prioritised targeted 
FX and regulatory measures.  The 
sequencing suggests a deliberate effort 
to distinguish actions aimed at 
preserving currency stability from those 
intended to achieve domestic monetary 
objectives, with rates remaining a 
potential, though not immediate, tool in 
the defence framework.

However, global developments have 
altered that dynamic . Rising US yields 
and a gradual increase in the US Fed 
Funds rate (DBS baseline: a total of 75bp 
hikes) have compressed IN -US spreads. 
The recent sell -off in INR benchmark

### Page 6

India: Degrees of rupee defence October 16, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

bonds has effectively widened Gsec -
UST spreads across maturities, in 
contrast to the compression witnessed in 
regional high-yielders.

The latest announcements may not 
fully quell speculation over further 
policy action.

Upcoming measures might include -

-Markets anticipate relief on the long -
term capital gains tax structure on 
equities to draw in longer-duration 
inflows. This measure will boost inflows 
if it coincides with other factors that 
make domestic equities attractive for 
thematic investing , as global risk -free 
rates moderate.

-Street expectations also include steps 
to boost inward dollar repatriation by 
exporters to boost domestic dollar 
supply.

-Wider policy rate corridor akin to 2013.

-Policy rate response. While this might 
not be a panacea, it is a likely necessary 
condition. Raising the neutral rate might 
have mixed success in attracting capital 
market inflows ; though rate-sensitive 
flows are likely to get a fillip as investors 
seek attractive rate differentials in the 
region.

An adequate real rate cushion would 
also align with the evolving domestic 
inflation realities, which are expected to 
accelerate to 6% in the Oct-Dec quarter 
and remain above 5% well into 2027.

Given fluid global developments, we 
outline three scenarios on the rate path 
ahead. Our baseline assumption  is for 
75bp hikes in this cycle  (including Oct), 
spread between the Dece mber and 
February 2027 meetings. We don’t rule 
out an intermeeting rate hike before the 
December meeting, contingent on 
rupee movements.

Under the second scenario , s hould 
depreciation pressures on the rupee 
return, the 50bp hike might be brought 
forward to December , with another in 
February, taking cumulative hikes in this 
cycle to 100bp. The t hird scenario  is a 
more aggressive 150bp worth of hikes in 
this cycle, with 50bp front -loaded in 
December, and the rest spilling into 2027. 
Factors that  will determine whether 
these scenarios materialise are the 
resumption and scale of rupee 
depreciation, oil price direction, US Fed 
Funds rate increases (DBSf: total 75bps) 
and other risk-off events.

Path ahead

Despite these measures, we see limited 
room for sustained INR appreciation on 
grounds of manufacturing/ trade 
competitiveness. A challenging flows 
portfolio flows outlook leaves the d oor 
open to further gradual rupee 
depreciation subject to global 
developments, with high sensitivity to oil 
price movements and risk sentiments . A 
strong rate response will help introduce  
favourable rate differentials and reduce 
currency volatility.

## Document 404: US Equity Views_ Over-earning, but not an earnings bubble

Source PDF: all report pdf/US Equity Views_ Over-earning, but not an earnings bubble.pdf

semiconductor share prices have recently lagged the trajectory of near-term EPS
estimates. Similarly, the market trades at a P/E discount relative to what a macro model

### Page 3

The magnitude of S&P 500 “over-earning”

S&P 500 earnings grew by an extraordinary 51% year/year in Q2 2026 and have 
grown by 26% during the past four quarters. During the last 30 years, the recent pace 
of earnings growth has been exceeded only during the post-recession rebounds in 2010 
and 2021.

The recent strength of S&P 500 earnings growth has raised investor concerns that 
current earnings power is unsustainable and that the market is in an “earnings 
bubble.” S&P 500 earnings have recently outpaced both their historical trend and their 
historical relationship with US GDP growth. S&P 500 4-quarter EPS growth has averaged 
7% during the past 30 years, and the relationship between S&P 500 earnings and US 
economic growth in recent decades would have pointed to a similar rate of earnings 
growth during the last few quarters.

### Page 4

The degree to which the US equity market is currently “expensive” depends in large 
part on the sustainability of recent earnings strength. The exceptional recent 
strength of earnings has driven an unusual wedge between equity valuation multiples 
based on near-term earnings and valuations on trend earnings. Because equity prices 
have failed to keep pace with surging earnings, near-term valuations show no hint of a 
bubble: The S&P 500 12-month P/E ratio has declined from 23x a year ago to 19x today 
and now matches its 10-year average. However, even an “average” multiple may be 
expensive if current earnings are unsustainable. The cyclically-adjusted P/E based on 
trailing 10-year earnings has climbed to one of its highest levels on record, falling shy of 
the highs of 1999-2000 but surpassing the level reached in 2021. Similarly, our 
colleagues have argued that the increase in market value of AI companies during the last 
few years requires an optimistic estimate of the future magnitude of AI-related profits 
and who will capture them.

### Page 5

The AI boom and S&P 500 earnings growth

Affirming investor concerns that US companies are “over-earning,” there are three 
factors that are boosting earnings today but will contribute to deceleration going 
forward: 1) AI capex spending, 2) semiconductor margin expansion, and 3) earnings 
from private investment gains.

AI capex 
The AI investment boom is driving nearly half of S&P 500 EPS growth this year, but 
its contribution will fade going forward. The mega-cap US hyperscalers are on track to 
spend $800 billion on capex this year, an increase of 94% vs. 2025. That spending is 
flowing through to the earnings of the AI infrastructure complex including 
semiconductor, tech hardware, industrials, and utilities companies, which are collectively 
accounting for roughly half of consensus S&P 500 earnings growth this year. The AI 
boom is also having a secondary impact on earnings outside of the infrastructure 
complex, for example by boosting capital markets activity and consumer wealth. Both 
consensus and GS analyst forecasts show hyperscaler capex growing at a slower rate in 
coming years, which should result in decelerating earnings growth for much of the AI 
infrastructure complex and a fading tailwind to S&P 500 earnings.

Hyperscaler depreciation expenses will continue to increase as capex growth 
decelerates, further dampening the boost of AI investment spending to S&P 500 
earnings growth. We estimate a drag from hyperscaler depreciation expenses on S&P 
500 earnings growth of 5pp in 2027, offsetting nearly half of the 11pp boost to earnings 
from capex spending. By 2028, the drag from depreciation should offset the S&P 500 
earnings uplift from continued capex spending.

### Page 7

If capex were fully expensed, consensus 2027 S&P 500 EPS would be 22% lower than 
under current accounting rules. The current gap between actual reported and 
hypothetical fully-expensed earnings is one of the widest in recent decades, exceeded 
only as earnings fell more quickly than capex during the recessions of 2001 and 2008.

Hyperscaler capex has consistently surprised relative to consensus estimates during 
the last few years, and the potential for additional surprises going forward creates a 
wide range of potential S&P 500 earnings outcomes. Our equity analysts expect 
hyperscaler capex will total $1.2 trillion in 2027 and $1.4 trillion in 2028, in which case 
the current tailwind from AI capex to S&P 500 EPS will fade by 2028. Exhibit 10 below 
outlines upside and downside scenarios around that base case. Next year, a $250 billion 
surprise in hyperscaler capex — in either direction — would shift S&P 500 EPS growth by 
about 6 pp in the same direction.  
We estimate that hyperscaler capex would need to decline by roughly 30%, to $570 
billion, to offset earnings growth for the rest of the S&P 500 in 2027. This decline 
would reduce earnings for the AI infrastructure complex by about 40%, leaving their 
profits about 25% above 2025 levels.

Semiconductor margin expansion 
A sharp increase in profit margins for many semiconductor companies has 
contributed to the above-trend recent pace of S&P 500 earnings growth. The 
combination of strong demand and limited supply has boosted semiconductor prices 
and gross margins, with the most pronounced margin expansion among memory stocks. 
S&P 500 memory firms currently enjoy gross margins of roughly 80%, more than double 
their historical average. Across the semiconductor industry, gross margins sit at their 
highest level in decades, and consensus expects margins to remain at similar levels 
through 2028.

Margin expansion has accounted for a large share of recent semiconductor earnings 
growth, but that boost should fade going forward. We estimate that about 1/4 of S&P 
500 Semiconductor earnings growth in 2026 is being driven by gross margin expansion. 
Our equity analysts believe the balance of memory supply and demand is likely to 
remain tight through 2027, with incremental supply additions unlikely to impact industry 
margins until 2028 at the earliest. However, they expect the rate of incremental margin 
expansion to slow meaningfully next year, reducing the contribution of semiconductors

### Page 10

“Other income” from private investment gains 
Appreciating equity investment stakes are also temporarily inflating S&P 500 
earnings. Mega-cap tech earnings have recently been boosted by large GAAP “other 
income” related to their equity investments. In Q2 2026, the mega-cap tech companies 
enjoyed unrealized investment gains in private companies that totaled roughly $150 
billion, translating into 12% of S&P 500 EPS. We expect H2 2026 reports will include 
additional “other income” that should be larger than the gains in Q1 but smaller than Q2 
totals. We expect a much smaller contribution in 2027. The complete removal of this 
“other income” next year would create a drag of 8 pp on S&P 500 earnings growth in 
2027 relative to 2026, all else equal.

### Page 11

S&P 500 earnings outlook

Our baseline forecast is for S&P 500 EPS to rise to $415 in 2027 (+11% year/year) 
and to $460 in 2028 (+11%). We expect an above-consensus base of earnings in 2026 
due to further “other income” gains from private equity stakes in H2 of this year. 
Excluding the “other income” inflating earnings in 2026, S&P 500 earnings growth next 
year would register 18%. Our 2027 EPS forecast is similar to the bottom-up consensus 
estimate of $419 but above the median top-down strategist estimate of $403.

### Page 12

Our 2028 EPS forecast is about 5% below the bottom-up consensus, signaling a 
return to the typical historical pattern of downward revisions to consensus earnings 
estimates after the last two years of rising estimates. Outside of the complex of AI 
capex beneficiaries, we expect sales growth will register close to nominal GDP , margins 
will expand modestly, and a growing boost from AI productivity will result in earnings 
growth of 9% in both 2027 and 2028.  Our forecasts for EPS growth among this group of 
companies are roughly 4-5% below consensus estimates in both 2027 and 2028, similar 
to the historical average magnitude of downward revision to consensus estimates. While 
AI investment spending has had a direct impact on the earnings of semiconductor and 
other AI infrastructure stocks, it has also lifted earnings for companies outside of the “ AI 
complex,” including by boosting capital markets activity as well as supporting consumer 
spending via the wealth effect. 
Solid GDP growth and easing pressure from energy prices will be the main macro 
tailwinds for earnings growth in 2027. Economic growth is the primary variable in our 
top-down earnings model and explains more than half of the historical variation in S&P 
500 EPS growth. Our economists expect US real GDP growth will average 2.1% in 2027 
and 2.3% in 2028. Our commodity strategists expect Brent crude oil will decline to 
$80/bbl by the end of 2027, which would weigh modestly on Energy sector profits but 
boost earnings for most other sectors. Outside of the AI-related risks outlined above, 
persistent input cost pressures and risks to the consumer from equity market volatility 
are two of the key macro downside risks to our earnings forecast.

### Page 13

As the earnings tailwind from AI investment spending fades, the trajectory of S&P 
500 profitability in coming years will depend increasingly on the realization of AI 
productivity gains. Our forecasts embed a 1 pp boost to S&P 500 EPS growth from AI 
productivity in 2027 and a 2 pp boost in 2028. Economy-wide surveys and corporate 
commentary signal enterprise adoption that is increasing but remains in early stages, 
and the impact of AI adoption on corporate earnings still appears narrow. However, the 
recent acceleration in enterprise AI spending suggests that the earnings impact of AI 
adoption should become clearer in coming quarters.

### Page 14

Valuing S&P 500 earnings

Like our forecasts and consensus estimates, market pricing suggests investors 
anticipate a deceleration in earnings growth going forward. This is true both for the 
broad market and for AI infrastructure stocks. For example, the share prices of 
semiconductor stocks have historically mirrored the path of consensus EPS estimates, 
and the recent moderation of semiconductor returns has aligned with consensus 
estimates for decelerating growth in 2027 and 2028.

The current S&P 500 P/E multiple signals an optimistic outlook but market 
skepticism regarding the sustainability of current profits. In addition to rising interest 
rates, an anticipated deceleration in earnings helps explain the recent decline in the S&P 
500 forward P/E multiple. Along with macro factors like interest rates, the level of 
corporate profitability has helped explain the variation in S&P 500 valuation over time. 
Based on the historical relationship between S&P 500 ROE and P/E, the current S&P 500 
ROE of 24% — a record high — would point to a forward 12-month P/E of over 21x 
against today’s macro backdrop. Given current inflation and interest rates, today’s S&P 
500 multiple of 19x would be consistent with an ROE of roughly 22%, more than 200 bp 
below the current level but otherwise matching 2021 as the highest on record.

### Page 15

Earnings, rather than valuations, should continue to drive equity market upside 
from here. Our 3-, 6-, and 12-month S&P 500 return targets are +5 (8000), +9% (8300), 
and +14% (8700). These imply a P/E multiple on consensus forward EPS that remains 
close to the current level of 19x. Our economists and interest rate strategists expect 
interest rates to decline modestly in coming quarters, creating some macro upside risk to 
equity valuations. However, we expect investor uncertainty regarding the impact of AI 
on the outlook for long-term profits to persist in coming quarters, making a dramatic 
increase in valuations unlikely.

## Document 405: US MARKETS CALL_ Still Proceeding Cautiously

Source PDF: all report pdf/US MARKETS CALL_ Still Proceeding Cautiously.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-24
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: US MARKETS CALL: Still Proceeding Cautiously
Date:    3 hours ago
US MARKETS CALL: Still Proceeding Cautiously

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US MARKETS CALL: Still
Proceeding Cautiously
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 24 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Jordan Christian / Unsplash

On September 15, we advised investors to proceed with caution. We
pushed out our 8,400 S&P 500 target from year-end 2026 to mid-2027
and set 7,900 as our new year-end target. On October 14, we reiterated
our caution. Sure enough, the stock market has been turbulent since mid-
September, with AI-related stocks falling sharply.

### Page 2

We are seeing some buying opportunities, but better ones are likely after
the November 3 midterm elections. Our main concern is a likely escalation
of the Middle East war. That could push oil prices above their $120 peak
earlier this year.
I. Stock Market Performance
The S&P 500 is still up double digits ytd at 11.4% (chart). XMAGS is up
13.1%, while MAGS is up only 6.2%. The gap has widened again in recent
weeks as MAGS rolled over. Meta led the latest leg down, falling 9.4% last

week after reporting earnings.
Breadth remained on the weak side. Only 37.8% of S&P 500 companies
are outperforming the index on a y/y basis (chart).

### Page 3

Rotation was notable last week. The S&P 500 Energy and Health Care
sectors led with gains of 1.5% each (chart). Communication Services fell
6.2%, and Industrials dropped 1.9%. As bad as the week felt, the S&P 500
was down only 0.5%.
II. S&P 500 Earnings
S&P 500 forward earnings per share rose to a record $414.41 during the
week of October 22 (chart). It is converging on the analysts’ 2027
consensus estimate of $424.56, up from this year's estimate of $365.94

(chart).
The 2026 consensus is closing in on our $375 per-share forecast (chart).
The consensus for 2027 matches our $425 forecast. The 2028 consensus
is already $493.32, well above our $450 forecast for that year and almost

### Page 4

at our $500 target for 2030! If the analysts are right, the S&P 500 could
rise to 10,000 by the end of next year. We are sticking with that target for
the end of 2029. We think the analysts are too bullish on earnings.
Forward earnings for the S&P 400 and S&P 600 are also at record highs
(chart).
III. Valuation
Valuation multiples continue to compress despite strong earnings (chart).
The Magnificent-7's forward P/E is 22.8, down from a 2026 peak of 28.5.

The S&P 500, S&P 400, and S&P 600 trade at 18.4, 14.5 and 14.0,
respectively.

### Page 5

The big valuation story is that for the S&P 500 Information Technology
sector. Its forward P/E dropped from 26.5 at the end of 2025 to 19.0 now
(chart). The S&P 500 has fallen from 22.2 to 18.6.
The S&P 500's forward earnings is up 31.0% ytd (chart). The index is up
11.4%. The forward P/E is down 13.5%. Stocks have gotten significantly
cheaper this year.

### Page 6

IV. Bonds
The 10-year Treasury yield is back in the “old normal” range of
4.00%-5.00% (chart). It peaked at 5.35%, attracting many buyers. It is
now at 4.96%. Some of that decline reflects safe-haven buying during the
recent risk-off selloff in the stock market. But market-based measures of
long-term inflation expectations remain subdued. Bond investors seem to
expect inflation to subside. We agree.
The 13-week change in the 10-year yield tends to track the Citigroup

Economic Surprise Index (CESI) (chart). Recently, the yield's change
overshot the index, as it has done before. Now, the 10-year yield is up
27bps over the past 13 weeks, down from over 80bps a few weeks ago.

### Page 7

Corporate credit default swap spreads are breaking out again (chart). The
widening reflects credit-quality concerns about companies like Oracle and
CoreWeave. The Bond Vigilantes are regulating credit in the AI
ecosystem. That's healthy. We aren't particularly concerned.

## Document 406: US SECTORS CALL_ Communication Services, Materials & Energy

### Page 1

Date:    7 hours ago
US SECTORS CALL: Communication Services, Materials & Energy

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
US SECTORS CALL:
Communication Services,
Materials & Energy
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 25 Oct 2026
View in browser
Photo by Noah Klassen / Unsplash

### Page 2

Investors spent the week worrying about an AI bubble. The S&P 500
slipped by 0.5%, led by a 6.2% drop in Communication Services (MW),
which was the worst-performing S&P 500 sector (table). Yet, Information
Technology fell just 0.3%. Energy (OW) led the winning sectors with a
1.6% gain, followed by Health Care (OW), up 1.5%.
Bubbles inflate valuation multiples. This year, multiples have contracted.
The S&P 500's forward P/E has fallen to 18.6 from 22.2 at the end of 2025

(chart). Information Technology's forward P/E is down to 19.0 from 26.5.
Stocks have gotten cheaper this year as earnings growth outpaced stock
price gains.
Here's more on the Communication Services, Materials, and Energy
sectors:

### Page 3

(1) Communication Services. Meta's Q3 report last Wednesday hit the
sector hard. Its free cash flow turned negative for the first time since its
2012 IPO, and it raised its 2026 capex guidance floor to $135 billion. Its
shares fell 7.0% and Interactive Media & Services fell 4.3% on Thursday,
closing down 7.0% for the week. Alphabet and Meta dominate the sector.
Together they account for 81.8% of its market cap and 70.0% of its
forward earnings (chart).

The sector's forward earnings is up 23.9% ytd, while its stock price index
is down 2.3% (chart). Its forward P/E has dropped to 17.2 from 22.1 at the
start of the year. That puts it below the S&P 500's 18.6.
Analysts expect earnings at Interactive Media & Services, the sector's
largest industry, to rise 72.3% this year, then fall 17.6% in 2027 (chart).
Yet they expect its revenues to grow 25.0% and 22.8% over the same two

### Page 4

years. Unrealized gains on Alphabet's stakes in private AI companies,
including Anthropic, inflated this year's earnings.
The de-rating isn't limited to AI. Netflix, which accounts for more than half
of Movies & Entertainment's market cap, reported Q3 results on Tuesday.
It cut its ad revenue outlook and guided Q4 earnings below expectations.
Its stock is now down 51% from its mid-2025 record high.
(2) Materials. Materials slipped 0.1% this week. Gold and Copper were

the sector's worst-performing industries, down 8.2% and 6.3% (chart).
Copper stocks fell along with the AI trade on Thursday.
Copper futures also fell slightly last week, ending at $6.48 per pound. The
AI selloff hit industrial metals on Thursday, but copper rebounded on
Friday. Rising inventories also weighed on the red metal last week.

### Page 5

Copper flowed back into London Metal Exchange warehouses, where
inventories rose to their highest since July. Traders spent the year
shipping copper to the US ahead of potential tariffs. The US price
premium that drove those flows has turned into a discount. Copper
remains near the top of an uptrend channel that began in 2016 (chart).
Forecasts have copper earnings rising 76.3% this year and 41.2% in
2027. The forward profit margin is up to 17.1%, the highest since 2022,

yet the forward P/E has dropped to 16.4. The selloff hit stock prices, not
earnings estimates.
Analysts expect Materials earnings to rise 40.2% this year and 11.8% in
2027. Materials accounts for just 1.9% of S&P 500 forward earnings and
only 1.6% of its market cap, the smallest share in over 30 years (chart).

### Page 6

We remain overweight. Materials offers rising earnings at a shrinking
multiple.
(3) Energy. Energy is up 55.1% ytd, the best-performing sector. Its
forward earnings has risen even faster, up 76.5% ytd to a record high
(chart). Energy still has the lowest forward P/E of the 11 sectors, at 13.8,
down from 15.7 at the start of the year.
Analysts see 2026 as the peak. They expect sector earnings to rise
101.9% this year, then fall 8.0% in 2027. Refining & Marketing shows this

most clearly. Its forward profit margin has soared to a record 9.0% (chart).
Its forward P/E is 9.0, with earnings forecast to fall 11.0% in 2027.

### Page 7

Oil & Gas Equipment & Services is the exception. Analysts expect its
earnings to fall 9.7% this year, then rise 25.2% in 2027 and 18.9% in 2028
(chart).
Consensus estimates assume the oil premium fades, and we expect it will
eventually. Renewed strikes on Iran after the midterms, which the US and
Israel reportedly have planned, would challenge the fading-premium
assumption. At only 3.7% of the S&P 500's market cap and a forward P/E
of 13.8, Energy is a cheap hedge against more geopolitical turmoil.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
In November 2025, we introduced Westpac-Now (see here),
a continuously-updated measure of current activity in the
Australian economy. Westpac-Now draws on more than
60 high frequency economic and financial variables, using
advanced statistical techniques to provide a timely pulse

check on economic momentum. It is the first Australian
model to incorporate internal banking data, enhancing its
predictive power.
This note provides the second estimate of GDP growth for the
September quarter 2026, using domestic and international
data released up to and including the September Labour Force
Survey (released 15 October 2026). Updates are published
monthly (see Appendix A for the release schedule).
Westpac Monthly Activity Index: activity falters as

headwinds mount
After showing tentative signs of improvement through the
second half of 2025, the Westpac Monthly Activity Index has
weakened markedly over 2026. The Index moved back into
negative territory in February and has declined further since.
While there were some signs of stabilisation around mid-year,
this proved to be short lived with the Index falling to –0.88 in
September, outside of the pandemic period, the weakest read
since 2013.
The economy is coming under pressure from multiple

fronts, with key headwinds coming from higher interest
rates, renewed disruptions to global energy markets and a
deepening in the housing downturn. Among households,
spending held up surprisingly well through the first half of the
year despite historically weak sentiment, but more recently
Growth momentum on shaky ground
spending outside of fuel has started to cool (see here). At
the same time, the labour market has continued to soften,
with unemployment reaching a cycle high of 4.7% (see here).

Meanwhile, the business sector is also showing signs of
softening, with sentiment deteriorating and conditions holding
at weak levels (see here). Business credit growth remains
a notable exception, although even here there are tentative
signs of easing (see here).
This deterioration is particularly evident in our heatmap.
Negative contributions have become both broader and more
pronounced through 2026, with the weakness intensifying
in August and September. Labour and business condition

indicators are now the largest sources of weakness, while
spending and confidence have also deteriorated materially.
Credit and capacity utilisation indicators have been
comparatively less negative, but there are currently few areas
providing a meaningful offset to the broader softening.
Our indicator heatmap decomposes changes in the Monthly
Activity Index into group-level contributions, allowing us to
assess which components are driving movements in activity.

For ease of interpretation, indicators are grouped into broad
categories (see the Appendix for further details). For example,
the labour market category includes both official ABS Labour
Force Survey outcomes and timelier survey-based measures of
labour demand and job advertisements.
Westpac-Now: Resilient growth outlook slowing not
stalling
Recall that Westpac-Now is a refined indicator that combines
our Monthly Activity Index with advanced econometric

techniques to estimate GDP growth. As previously noted,
the fit is not expected to be exact. Measured GDP includes
Neha Sharma
Economist

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
3
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
components that are not well captured by high-frequency
activity indicators, such as imputed rents for owner-occupiers,
housing-related ownership transfer costs, public and business
inventories, and depreciation.
To bridge monthly and quarterly data, we use a MIDAS

framework, allowing us to combine mixed-frequency inputs
and incorporate partial information for the current quarter
when producing GDP forecasts.
Based on current model estimates, Westpac-Now’s second
estimate for Q3 2026 points to GDP growth of 0.5%qtr
(with a 70% confidence interval of –0.1%qtr to 1.0%qtr)
and 2.1%yr, representing a slight downgrade from the first
estimate of +0.6%qtr but still a step up from the 0.4%qtr
(2.1%yr) outcome recorded in Q2 2026. While aggregate

growth appears to have held up through Q3, the underlying
picture became softer as the quarter progressed. At the
second decimal place the estimate is 0.46%qtr, leaving it
close to rounding down to 0.4%qtr.
With several headwinds now bearing on the economy
simultaneously, there is a growing risk that the slowdown in
activity becomes more pronounced than currently anticipated.
At the same time, the strength of the business investment
upswing provides an important counterweight that should

help cushion the broader slowdown.
Looking ahead, model dynamics suggest growth will tick
down to 0.4%qtr in Q4 2026 before picking up in Q1 2027.
However, these projections remain much more tentative,
given their reliance on projected inputs rather than realised
data. In addition, heightened uncertainty, particularly around
the renewed tensions in the Middle East conflict, means risks
around these forward estimates are larger than usual.
September Quarter data: a softer end to the quarter

Data released in the first month of the September quarter
indicated that the economy has picked up momentum relative
to the previous quarter, with our first nowcast estimating Q3
growth at 0.6%qtr, in line with the 0.6%qtr “pure forecast”
based solely on data available up to June 2026.
However, the flow of data since then shows downside risks to
growth have begun to materialise. Incorporating the August,
followed by the September (to date), data both yielded

nowcasts of 0.5%qtr (with a slight softening at the second
decimal place). This contrasts with Q2 2026, when weakness
emerged early in the quarter, in April, before subsequent data
pointed to stabilisation.
Note that monthly activity estimates are more uncertain
than normal when inflation is shifting. Faster price growth
can cause nominal indicators, such as consumer spending,
to rise more strongly even though ‘real’, inflation-adjusted
spending may be softening (and vice versa). The inclusion

of ‘real’ indicators, such as employment and banking
transaction volumes, helps mitigate some – but not all – of this
uncertainty.
Bottom line:
Westpac-Now points to GDP growth of around 0.5%qtr
(2.1%yr) in Q3 2026, suggesting aggregate growth held up
reasonably well through the quarter, although the flow of
data points to softer momentum as the quarter progressed.
The substantial upswing in business investment remains
an important source of support, helping to offset mounting

weakness elsewhere in the economy.
Looking ahead, the accumulation of headwinds is expected to
see growth slow further towards year-end, with Westpac-Now
currently pointing to growth of around 0.4%qtr in Q4 2026.
Downside risks around the outlook have increased, but for now
our central case remains one of slower growth rather than a
material downturn.

## Document 419: Who finances private credit in the United States_ What the Federal Reserve’s data reveal (2026-10-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Who finances private credit in the United States_ What the Federal Reserve’s data reveal (2026-10-28).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-28
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

28 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
WHO FINANCES PRIVATE CREDIT IN THE UNITED STATES? WHAT THE FEDERAL RESERVE’S DATA REVEAL
Céline Choulet
SOURCE: FEDERAL RESERVE (ENHANCED FINANCIAL ACCOUNTS), BNP PARIBAS
The private credit market raises many questions, and its lack of transparency is not helping. Official public data remain scarce. The available
estimates come mainly from private sources, whose methodologies vary widely. The Financial Accounts of the Federal Reserve (Fed) now shed

new light on the matter. They reveal that private credit finances 7.1% of US corporate debt through households (31% of funds’ total financing),
insurers and pension funds (20%), and banks (19%).
Private credit can take several forms. The most common is loans granted by closed-end funds 1 to small and medium-sized, unlisted and highly
indebted companies. These loans are generally variable-rate, with flexible terms based on cash flow rather than a measure of profitability. While

this asset class has inherent vulnerabilities 2 (Bank of France, 2025), it is also particularly well-suited to financing innovative and long-term
projects (such as digital 3, infrastructure, renewable energy and healthcare), especially when combined with an equity stake in the company. Its
slower growth in Europe likely explains, in part, the region’s lag in innovation financing.
Private credit finances 7.1% of US corporate debt…
Private credit has grown rapidly in the United States over the past decade and has established itself as an alternative to bank loans for

a category of borrowers. It now accounts for 17.3% of the total outstanding loans to American non-financial corporations (NFCs) 4 and 7.1%
1 Strictly speaking, a closed-end fund has a fixed term during which investors cannot withdraw their invested capital. However, the sector has diversified since 2020. The Fed distinguishes
between 1) strictly closed-end private funds (private debt funds), 2) business development companies, which may have an indefinite lifespan and allow for occasional redemptions of

shares, and 3) interval and tender offer funds, which offer the possibility of scheduled redemptions.
2 Notably, high credit risk, high leverage, difficulty in valuing loans, opaque credit ratings, occasionally complex credit structures, high concentration within the sector and increasing
liquidity risk.
3 In 2025, nearly half of the direct loans made by private credit funds went to the tech sector (software, data centres and artificial intelligence) (Abbassi et al., 2026).

4 No comparable data exist for the Eurozone. According to France Invest (2026) data, private credit issued by French funds accounted for 4% of annual bank credit flows to NFCs in 2025.
Who finances US private credit funds?

### Page 2

2
The bank for
a changing
world
Chart of the Week / 28 October 2026 economic-research.bnpparibas.com
of their debt. Approximately one-third (35%) is managed by strictly closed-end funds, with another third (36%) by semi-open-end funds
that allow occasional early redemptions. The remaining third is held on the balance sheets of non-resident private debt funds (25%) and
securitisation vehicles (4%).
… through banks, insurers and pension funds, and households

In yet another example of the lengthening of the credit intermediation chain in the United States (following that of the mortgage market),
banks are among the main creditors of private credit funds (65% of their debt and 19% of their total financing). The low risk weighting
of loans granted by banks to these vehicles, compared to that of direct bank loans to non-financial corporations, is undoubtedly a factor
(Chernenko et al., 2024). The holdings of insurers and pension funds – consisting mainly of shares in strictly closed-end funds – make

them among the leading investors in private debt funds (30% of issued shares and 20% of total financing). This asset class offers them the
opportunity to diversify their portfolios and attractive returns. The rise of semi-open-end funds has expanded the investor base to include
retail investors (31% of total financing), albeit at the cost of increased liquidity risk, as evidenced by the significant wave of redemptions in
late 2025 and early 2026 5. American households directly hold 70% of the equity issued by semi-open funds and 22% of the shares issued

by strictly closed funds 6.
Abbassi, P ., Aldasoro, I., and Doerr, S. (2026), Financing the digital economy: the role of private credit, BIS Quarterly Review, September
2026
Banque de France (2025), The reshaping of the credit landscape in light of the development of private debt and Its implications for
financial stability, Financial Stability Report, December 2025
Chernenko, S., Ialenti, R. and Scharfstein, D. (2024), Bank capital and the growth of private credit, Working paper

France Invest (2026), Private debt fund activity in France, March 2026

5 Triggered by concerns regarding private credit funds’ high exposure to the software sector and the quality of the loans granted.
6 This exposure is likely overestimated because, in the Fed’s financial accounts, the household sector includes individuals and non-profit institutions, as well as resident private
equity funds, which form partnerships with private credit funds. In order to align more closely with the European definition, households here include sole proprietors.

利差優勢消長，新興與商品
貨幣走勢各異
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
高油價、美元偏強及全球景氣分化持續牽動新興與
商品貨幣。澳洲就業轉弱、通膨改善，澳洲央行升
息週期料暫告一段落，澳幣短線料區間盤整。紐西
蘭央行意外按兵不動削弱紐幣短期動能，但升息預
期仍提供支撐。加拿大央行政策轉鷹及高油價利好
加幣，惟經濟與貿易不確定性限制升值空間。中國
內需偏弱，但出口暢旺、企業結匯需求強，加上政
策對升值容忍度提高，人民幣料溫和升值。南非幣
受美元、高油價及利差收窄壓抑，但財政紀律改善、
信用前景轉佳及實質利率優勢有助緩和貶值壓力，
料維持區間震盪。
市場投資展望
資產 4Q26看法 投資展望

紐幣 NZDUSD 0.59 美元強勢壓抑紐元表現，惟中期復甦動能仍在
加幣 USDCAD 1.39 央行轉鷹支撐加幣，惟經濟及貿易風險限制升值空間
人民幣 USDCNY 6.65 人民幣迎結匯需求與中美利差擴大的拉鋸
南非幣 USDZAR 16.20 南非幣短線承壓，利差優勢支撐中期前景

《投資關鍵 – 澳洲景氣放緩、澳央轉向觀望，預期
澳幣盤整》
隨著國際油價維持高位，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至 4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。
但隨著高利率與高通膨壓力持續拖累經濟動能，近期
公布的 9月就業意外減少 3.93萬人 、失業率更升至
4.7%，已連續兩個月落在澳央主席認為可有效壓抑
通膨的失業率4.5~5.0%區間；通膨方面，9月份澳洲
CPI雖由4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以
住房通膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本
輪升息暫時告終 ，2027年政策轉向觀望，如國際油
價回落至90美元以下，澳央有降息空間。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不

振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有
限，加以預期聯準會在 12月再度升息 ，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整，要進一步突破或要待2Q27美國
通膨回落、降息預期再度升溫，方有走揚力量。
重點摘要
• 經濟基本面降溫 ，核心CPI穩
定，澳洲央行暫停升息轉向
觀望 ，澳幣維持在 0.7~0.71
中性盤整。

紐西蘭通膨升溫訊號浮現，月度物價年增 5.2%
《投資關鍵 - 央行意外按兵不動拖累紐元 ，後續升息
預期提供下檔支撐》
過去一個月 ，受美元強勢 、美債殖利率及油價走高拖
累，紐幣兌美元一度下探 0.5565，其後隨升息預期升
溫而回穩 。惟紐西蘭央行 10月28日意外維持 OCR於
2.75%不變，促使市場下修短期升息預期 ，紐幣再度
承壓。
紐西蘭經濟延續溫和復甦 ，企業信心及農產品價格改
善，惟勞動市場仍有閒置產能 。雖然整體通膨低於預
期，但核心通膨及價格廣度上升 ，加上油價高企與紐

幣偏弱，我們預期4Q26通膨仍具黏性，價格壓力擴散
風險猶存。
我們仍維持偏鷹派的政策基準情境 。10月以4比3票按
兵不動，較反映升息時點後移 ，而非整體政策路徑轉
向，緊縮週期尚未結束。我們仍預期12月及明年3月具
升息空間 ，尤其若通膨 、油價及匯率壓力持續 ，政策
立場料維持偏緊。
展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動削弱紐幣利差支撐，我
們仍看好紐元中期修復空間 ，惟短期上行動能有所減
弱。升息預期降溫 ，加上大選 、美元及全球風險情緒
等不確定性，料使匯價維持較高波動。進入1Q27後，
隨政策不確定性逐步下降 ，經濟溫和復甦有望成為紐
幣主要支撐 。考量匯價已反映相當程度的成長疑慮及
外部利空 ，若基本面持續改善 ，紐幣中期仍具修復空
間。
• 展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動
削弱紐幣利差支撐 ，我們仍看
好紐元中期修復空間 ，惟短期
上行動能有所減弱。
• 隨政策不確定性逐步下降 ，經
濟溫和復甦有望成為紐幣主要
支撐 。考量匯價已反映相當程
度的成長疑慮及外部利空 ，若
基本面持續改善 ，紐幣中期仍

具修復空間。

### Page 4

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵 - 央行轉鷹支撐加幣，惟經濟及貿易風險
限制升值空間》
能源價格高企令加拿大通膨面臨上行風險，加拿大央
行政策預期轉鷹亦推動加美利差劣勢收窄，支持加幣
自10月中旬低點反彈 。不過，隨本次25個基點升息
已大致反映於市場定價，加幣能否延續升勢將更取決
於央行對後續緊縮路徑的指引，以及市場對終端利率
的重新定價，而非本次升息本身。
加拿大央行最新升息 25個基點至2.5%，為逾三年來
首次升息，並表示若能源價格進一步推升廣泛通膨壓
力，不排除繼續收緊政策 。央行亦將 2027年通膨預
測由 2.0%上調至 3.1%，並將今年經濟成長預測由
0.7%上調至1.0%，短期政策訊號偏鷹 。惟核心通膨
仍相對溫和，勞動市場、製造業需求及招聘意願亦呈
轉弱跡象，意味若經濟活動進一步降溫，持續升息空
間或有限。高油價及偏高的投機性加幣空頭部位仍構
成短期利好，但美加貿易不確定性可能繼續拖累企業
投資及成長前景。因此，我們維持4Q26至1Q27加幣

中性看法。短期加美利差收窄及油價高企有望限制加
幣下行風險，但在增長前景改善或央行釋出更明確的
持續緊縮訊號前，加幣進一步升值空間有限。

美元兌加幣即期匯率（右軸）

### Page 5

，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要
• 從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國
際經貿局勢變化三大視角切
入，綜合判斷人民幣溫和升
值的看法不變。
《投資關鍵 – 政經拉鋸與景氣K型下，人民幣溫和升值》
中國公布最新 3Q26 GDP成長4.5%，出口年增率升至
28.4%，貿易順差由前季的3148.6億美元擴大至3651.2
億美元 。市場預估中國 2026年全年貿易順差將超越
2025年1.2兆美元紀錄，再創新高。惟中國外需繁榮卻
無法掩蓋內需疲軟困境 ，中國3Q26零售銷售年增率僅
0.7%，固定資產投資衰退7%。儘管中國陸續推出穩增
長政策，大多以財政政策為主軸，貨幣政策偏向定向式
而非全面降準降息。因此儘管美啟動升息，中美利差尚
保持穩定，因此內需疲軟與有限度的寬鬆貨幣政策對人
民幣走勢並未形成顯著壓力。
中國的出口暢旺除受惠科技週期貢獻外，強勁汽車出口
至歐洲也是主因，9月中國汽車出口維持62%的高成長。
惟加劇保護主義傾向，中歐達成初步協議，未來四年內
將對歐洲的混合動力車出口量減少一半以上，具體配額、

最低售價及豁免條件仍待協商。政策角度觀察，人民幣
升值本身可能成為中國應對外部壓力的重要工具，減緩
低估匯率促進出口的國際批評。
考量關稅可能成為攻防戰手段之一，研判中國廠商或搶
出口支持外貿表現高檔，同時官方可能對人民幣升值空
間容忍態度提高 ，疊加前三季結售匯順差高達 3,941億
美元，創2010年以來新高，顯示企業結匯意願強勁，持
續為人民幣提供支撐。從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國際經
貿局勢變化的三大視角切入，綜合判斷人民幣溫和升值
的看法不變。

不做任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行並不負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資之風險，並就投
資結果自行負責。未經本公司許可，本資料及訊息不得逕行抄錄、翻印或另作派發。本行以上市場資訊與分析，不涉及特定投資標的之建議。
資料來源：中國外匯管理局，2026/10，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10/27

### Page 6

《投資關鍵 - 基本面改善但部位趨擁擠，南非幣料維
持震盪》
南非幣第四季以來震盪偏弱，USD/ZAR近期回升至約
16.60。外部環境仍偏不利，高油價推升進口成本與
通膨壓力，加上美元偏強及美國南非利差收窄，削弱
南非幣套息吸引力。與此同時，巴西總統大選結果引
發市場波動，增添新興市場不確定性，短期投資人風
險偏好轉趨審慎，亦可能加重新興市場貨幣的資金流
出壓力。
基本面方面，南非財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉佳，
加上較高實質利率及具吸引力的利差，仍為南非幣提
供支撐。惟高油價及聖嬰現象相關極端天氣風險令通
膨壓力偏高，限制政策寬鬆空間。另一方面，近期投
機性及資產管理機構多頭部位明顯增加，顯示市場部
位漸趨擁擠。在外部風險升溫及風險偏好轉弱下，獲
利了結與部位調整可能放大短期波動，即使匯價回檔，
逢低布局的風險報酬吸引力仍相對有限。
整體而言，南非幣基本面有所改善，但短期外部環境
與部位面均偏不利。財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉
佳及實質利率優勢有助建立匯率下檔支撐，惟強勢美
元、高油價、利差收窄及偏擁擠的多頭部位限制升值

空間。預期4Q26美元兌南非幣仍以區間震盪為主，維
持南非幣中性看法。
重點摘要
資料來源：CFTC，Bloomberg，2026/9，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/10

【市場短評】
受擔憂中東地緣衝突恐升級推升通膨 ，金價曾跌破4,000美
元，後因美實質利率回落而反彈 ，美聯準會 10月雖暫停升
息，但美通膨數據居高不下 、市場權衡美伊衝突升級或降
溫，近五日金價反彈收斂至 0.9%。下周觀察美國採購經理
人指數 、職位空缺數 、初請失業金人數 、消費者信心及中
東地緣緊張對油價的影響 ，本行基準情境認為年底將升息
一次，明年3月除非美伊衝突上升才會升息 ，受高利率持續
期間較長 、美元走升加上中東地緣政治變數 ，短線金價走
勢震盪將放大測試3,800~4,000美元支撐。若實質利率確定
回落、擔憂債務可持續性 、分散交易對手風險及多元化資
產配置，中國人行連續 23個月購金 ，並加大購金步伐 ，預
期中期金價仍有機會緩升。

主題報告概述 : 本篇報告將分析 2026年美元現況 ，最終形成
2027年美元看法。以下為本篇報告內容概要:
(1) 匯市現況：美國伊朗戰爭導致通膨疑慮再起，美元走強。
(2) 政治議題：2026年共和黨失去絕對執政對美元的影響。
(3) 期中選後風險因子：財政、貿易戰、美伊戰爭。
(4) 美元基本面：景氣與通膨循環將維持在金髮女孩經濟環境。
(5) 美元技術面：技術面跌破年線，籌碼面偏多但8月轉弱。
(6) 非美匯市: 歐洲政治面利空及日本阻貶為美元提供底部支撐
匯市結論: 美元已從2026年初的降息交易轉向受到美伊戰爭 、

油價上漲及通膨預期升溫支撐 ，但此波強勢更多來自地緣政治
與高利率環境 ，而非美國基本面出現結構性轉強 。期中選舉後 ，
共和黨失去完全執政優勢 ，國會分裂將限制大規模財政擴張 ，
有助抑制通膨與赤字惡化 ，但也降低進一步刺激經濟成長的動
能。展望2027年，美國仍面臨債務上限 、財政協商、貿易政策、
美伊衝突及烏俄戰爭等風險 ，其中地緣政治與油價走勢仍是左
右美元的重要變數 。2027年美元並非進入新一輪強勢多頭週期 ，
而是在高利率支撐下維持區間整理 。2027年美國經濟基準情境
穩健，景氣溫和成長 ，通膨從高檔放緩 。若無重大地緣政治升
級事件，美元指數預期維持97–105區間震盪，並呈現「上有壓
力、下有支撐 」的盤整格局 。當地緣政治升溫時美元偏強 ，當
通膨回落與利率下降時美元偏弱。
資料來源： 中國信託商業銀行整理。2026/9

(1)匯市現況 : 美伊戰爭導致通膨疑慮再起，
美元走強
匯市現況: 2026年美元走勢主要受到美國經濟基本面、Fed貨幣
政策預期、川普政策以及地緣政治風險交互影響。
2026年初，川普重返貿易壁壘政策 、美國就業動能放緩且通膨
趨穩，市場普遍預期降息循環延續 ，使美元維持偏弱整理格局 ，
美元指數於1月27日一度跌至全年低點95.55。期間宣布華許出
任Fed主席的消息曾短暫帶動美元反彈 ，但整體仍未擺脫區間
震盪格局 。然而，2月28日伊朗事件爆發 ，3月9日西德州原油
一度接近每桶 120美元，能源價格上升帶動全球通膨預期升溫 ，
美國降息預期下降。進入3-4月，美國經濟數據進一步展現韌性，
製造業 、消費及投資表現改善 ，Fed季度預測亦同步上修 GDP

成長與通膨展望 。受此影響 ，利率期貨隱含年底前降息預期由
原先降息2碼逐步修正至不降息 ，推動美元於2Q26展開反彈。
同時 ，受油價飆升影響 ，9月Fed季度預測將今年 PCE上修至
3.7%，官員點陣圖亦上修利率路徑 ，市場開始討論通膨壓力可
能迫使Fed重新升息 。匯市焦點亦逐漸轉向新任聯準會主席華
許提出的新貨幣政策框架 。在Fed政策預期持續調整下 ，美元
指數於6月24日升至全年高點101.8，隨後進入高檔盤整格局。

重點提示
(2)政治議題：2026年共和黨失去絕對執政對
美元的影響(2/3)
數席次機率下行趨勢可見 ，當川普政策面無法政治上快速解決
問題並且影響民生時，取得參眾議院的機率加速下行。
期中選舉結果對美元影響 ：觀察1970年以來美國期中選舉結束
後一年美元之平均表現。
民主黨主導兩院 ：當民主黨期中選舉後在參眾議院均為多數 ，
美元指數平均下跌 -4.6%，6次有4次為下跌 ，且前一年美元均
下跌。1971、1987及2007年為下跌幅度最大的3年。
1971年尼克森(共和黨)總統宣布停止黃金兌換 ，10%進口附加
關稅，迫使盟友重啟貿易與貨幣談判(最終達成史密松寧協定)，
美元下跌-7.8%。

1987年雷根(共和黨)延續1985年廣場協議後美元貶值趨勢延續 。
雙赤字壓力 、通膨升溫 、利率上升以及外資拋售壓力導致發生
1987年10月19日黑色星期一股市崩盤 ，美元指數當年下跌 -
17.6%(1986年下跌-16.1%)。
2007年布希(共和黨)總統時期面臨雙赤字 、非美利差縮小 ，以
及次貸風暴風險升溫。美元下跌-8.3%(2006年下跌-8.2%)。

涉及特定投資標的之建議。
Thematic Report
重點提示
(2)政治議題：2026年共和黨失去絕對執政對
美元的影響(3/3)
共和黨、民主黨各主導一議院 ：當美國國會進入兩黨各主導一
議院時期，美元指數期中選舉過後一年平均上漲 3.6%，4次有3
次為上漲，且前一年4次美元均為上漲。美國期中選舉若造成國
會由不同政黨掌控 ，通常會限制總統隨意推動大規模擴張性財
政政策與赤字支出 。這類財政節制有助於降低通膨失控風險 ，
進而支撐美元走強。4次國會分裂時段其中較大美元升值趨勢為
1983年雷根(共和黨)時期。 1980年以來聯準會主席保羅伏克爾
採取強力升息手段打擊通膨 ，美國利率保持在相對高檔一段時
間且通膨已逐步受到控制 (從1980年15%降至4%以下)。美元

1983年升值14.9%(1982年升值12.1%)。1983年後，美國經濟
順利擺脫先前的嚴重衰退 ，展開強勁增長 ，市場對美國資產信
心大增，形成所謂強勢美元格局 。美元指數在80年代高點轉折
時期美國實質利率(基準利率-消費者物價指數)已從高檔下降。
小結：2026年期中選舉結果可能形成國會分裂 ，國會分裂長期
有助抑制赤字與通膨 ，因此長期對美元有利 ，但短期可能因預
算協商及債務上限僵局增加政治風險 ，對美元造成波動。2027
年將再度面臨美國債務上限 ，以及美伊地緣政治 、貿易戰的變
數均將牽動實質利率的變化 ，因此接下來將進一步分析選後影
響美元的關鍵因子。

Thematic Report
重點提示
(3)期中選後風險因子：財政、貿易戰、美伊戰
爭(1/3)
期中選舉過後影響美元的關鍵因子聚焦 財政面 (政府關門事件)、
貿易戰(法院裁定關稅無效後宣布以其他方式課徵關稅)、地緣政
治面(伊朗戰爭觸發油價上揚)。
財政面：8月國會通過持續撥款決議至 12月11日，代表政府關
門的風險將落在11月3日後，但新國會於2027年1月3日任職，
因此很可能選後再延後撥款決議 。除此之外 ，根據兩黨政策中
心預測，2027年9月後可能再度面臨美國債務上限議題 ，屆時
面臨2028年預算審核，參眾議院的分治恐延長達成共識的時間
以及提升美國主權債信評或展望調降風險 。從全國納稅人聯盟

(NTU)情境分析預測，美國聯邦政府收支很可能在2027年2月至
7月觸及債務上限。一旦債務達到法定限額，財政部長將宣布暫
停發行債務期 。在暫停發行債務期間內 ，財政部不得增加受法
定限額約束的債務 ，但到期債務仍可展期 。計算美國財政部透
過動用收入 、現有現金儲備和採取非常規措施 ，真正無法履行
聯邦義務約在2028年2月。然而債務上限和債務違約日(X-Date)
何時達到存在很大的不確定性 。受債務上限約束的債務將於
2027年3月接近法定上限 500億美元以內，之後隨著4月稅收的
到來而暫時下降 ，但若政府財政赤字增加超過原先預測 ，X-
Date將會提前到來。

Thematic Report
重點提示
(3)期中選後風險因子：財政、貿易戰、美伊戰
爭(2/3)
美國信評方面 ，惠譽8月將美國信評維持在 AA+評級且展望穩
定，但警告信評下調風險因子如下 ：政府債務佔GDP水準或償
債能力惡化 ，例如未能解決公共支出和收入的平衡 ，或遭受宏
觀金融衝擊 。經濟政策制定可信度下降導致削弱美元儲備貨幣
地位，從而降低政府融資彈性 。惠譽預期 ，美國債務占GDP比
重到2028年可能升至123%，較AA評級國家46.3%的債務比率
中值高出一倍以上 ，因此，美國信評或展望下修的風險有可能
同時伴隨財政惡化或經濟成長放緩 /衰退造成償債能力惡化 。
2027年美國財政議題將繼續面臨逆風 ，共和黨失去完全執政的

環境對財政有利但政治僵局則凸顯債務上限及違約的風險 ，因
此增加美元短期波動風險。
貿易戰：2026年川普貿易戰從對等關稅全面性課徵轉向法律攻
防，預期2027年貿易政策以各國與美國雙邊貿易協定談判為主
軸，以及針對進口品項實施不同的關稅 (川普8月宣布2027年起
對一系列加拿大汽車產品加徵最高達 50%的關稅)。川普貿易政
策不確定性因素延續 ，但本行認為時序進入 2027年貿易戰全面
性惡化的可能性已降低 ，且企業及投資人已對貿易政策不確定
性有一定程度的認知 ，因此傳導至資產價格的影響性亦將有所
降低。
地緣政治 ：地緣政治風險的擴散效應高於上述財政及關稅 。主
因荷姆茲海峽能源輸出量佔全球 20%，油價上漲對通膨的影響
為2027年美元走勢的主要變數 。2月底事件爆發以來原油遠期
曲線現貨溢價 ，代表短期內市場需求強烈或發生供給中斷 。目
前美伊戰爭從熱戰轉向封鎖荷姆茲海峽 。UAE及部分中東國家
已開始運用地面輸油管線避開該管線 ，但伊朗無人機打擊範圍
遠至含蓋紅海 ，倘若事件面惡化 ，紅海運輸除了葉門胡塞武裝

組織干擾，亦有伊朗無人機攻擊風險。目前美伊談判仍未達成
✓

Thematic Report
重點提示
(3)期中選後風險因子：財政、貿易戰、美伊戰
爭(3/3)
協議，伊朗拒絕交出高濃縮鈾 ，因此仍無法排除美國運用地面
部隊進行搜索 ，也無法排除伊朗透過遠端無人機攻擊美國在其
他中東國家的軍事或煉油設施 ，美國運用地面部隊恐將地緣政
治風險升級，屆時油價、利率以及通膨預期可能同步向上。
風險變數對2027年美元的影響 ：美國政府債務問題並非全新風
險因子。自1960年以來，美國國會已經累計調整法定債務上限
超過78次。然而，2027年若期中選後國會分裂 ，疊加年底預算
勢必面臨再延後撥款決議 ，屆時政府關門或違約的不確定性因
素將影響經濟成長並且降低聯準會緊縮的可能 。而美國債務上

限再度上調則可能引起信評展望調降的風險 。以美元的角度而
言，有紀律的財政計畫的確可將加強投資人對美元資產的信心 ，
對美元有利 。但短線若因此造成政府關門以及無法在預算達成
協議則對美元短線不利 ，因此定調2027年財政風險因子在分裂
國會環境，對美元而言屬於短空長多。
除了債務風險外 ，貿易戰及美伊戰爭事件的發展最終將反應在
油價及通膨的走勢。從今年油價一度飆升至每桶120美元可見，
短期油價的上漲並未造成短線通膨危機。但不可否認，高油價

(4)美元基本面：景氣與通膨循環將維持在金髮
女孩經濟環境(2/2)
若債務上限 、地緣政治 、貿易壁壘等議題未惡化 ，預期美國經
濟偏向溫和成長 ，通膨可望放緩 。觀察中國信託美國景氣動向
指數可見，3個月以來，股市動能略顯放緩，但多數領先指標仍
持續改善 。FactSet 預估2027年標普500企業獲利成長 15%，
且營收成長高達9%，企業獲利面維持高度擴張。樂觀的企業前
景繼續帶動資本支出 ，彭博社統計2027年標普500企業資本支
出年增率仍高達 20%，正向的企業獲利持續傳導到實際經濟 。
2024年以來，實際經濟數據持續改善 ，且今年已回到 0軸以上
擴張區間 。伴隨調查及價格指標持續向上 ，整體景氣動能趨勢

正向。 展望2027年，溫和成長伴隨通膨放緩美元處於盤整偏弱
格局，基本情境內貨幣政策則傾向維持基準利率穩定。

義大利民粹政黨執政時期10
年公債殖利率與歐元區利差
30%，歐洲政治生態將進入高度不穩定與兩極化的狀態 。在歐
洲多黨內閣制或雙首長制國家中 ，30%是傳統主流政黨能否順
利執政的關鍵臨界點 。進入2027年，歐盟將有 7國舉行國會選

舉，1月30日德國總統選舉以及4月18日法國總統大選。民粹主
義右翼政黨正在崛起 ，並可能在多個國家大幅增加席次 。每次
選舉結果都將影響各國政治局勢 ，進而反映整個歐洲的政治環
境。然而，右翼民粹主義的崛起對金融市場也並非絕對利空 ，
義大利右翼政黨取得政權後堅持財政紀律 ，向金融市場展現負
責任的治理 ，同時關注移民和文化衝突等社會議題 ，以維繫基
層支持者 。義大利和歐元區 10年期公債利差從義大利右翼政黨
勝選時2.45%至今已收斂至0.83%。此外，許多右翼民粹主義政
黨也緩和了其疑歐立場 ，不再主張退出歐元區或歐盟 。2027年
法國總統大選為首要政治議題。法國極右翼領袖勒龐(Le Pen)與
她所屬的政黨國民聯盟 (RN)近年來調整了政綱 ，已放棄脫歐主
張，並且在財政上淡化激進政策 ，強調財政紀律將遵守歐盟規
範，將財政赤字控制在GDP 3%以下。整體而言，歐洲政治光譜
進入兩極化 ，法國選後議會仍需籌組多數聯盟 ，財政惡化風險
可控，但若極左派梅蘭雄意外勝選，大幅增加政府支出、對富

Thematic Report
重點提示
(6)非美匯市 : 歐洲政治不確定性及日本阻貶為
美元提供底部支撐(5/5)
✓
日圓長期轉為多頭
關鍵因子則將落在
日本政府是否開始
運用政策引導海外
投資資金回流
可視為第一波技術面日圓多方關卡。就基本面而言，政府干預匯
市無法改變貨幣政策與財政政策的路徑。若日本通膨無法帶動日
本央行加速升息，日圓利率水準則無法形成資金流入的誘因。以
日圓長期轉為多頭關鍵因子則將落在日本政府是否開始運用政策
引導海外投資資金回流(日本財務省統計到2025年底，日本在海
外的淨資產達近562兆日圓)，將是長期影響日圓走勢的最有效政
策。
匯市結論: 美元已從2026年初的降息交易轉向受到美伊戰爭、油

價上漲及通膨預期升溫支撐 ，但此波強勢更多來自地緣政治與
高利率環境 ，而非美國基本面出現結構性轉強 。期中選舉後 ，
共和黨失去完全執政優勢 ，國會分裂將限制大規模財政擴張 ，
有助抑制通膨與赤字惡化 ，但也降低進一步刺激經濟成長的動
能。展望2027年，美國仍面臨債務上限 、財政協商、貿易政策
及美伊衝突等風險 ，其中地緣政治與油價走勢仍是左右美元的
重要變數 。外部變數則關注烏俄戰爭是否停戰 、美日聯和干預
日圓與日本2028財年赤字規模 ，以及法國總統大選對歐洲政治
光譜的影響。
整體而言，2027年美元並非進入新一輪強勢多頭週期 ，而是在
高利率支撐下維持區間整理 。2027年美國經濟基準情境穩健 ，
景氣溫和成長 ，通膨從高檔放緩 。若無重大地緣政治升級事件 ，
美元指數預期維持 97-105區間震盪，並呈現「上有壓力、下有
支撐」的盤整格局 。當地緣政治升溫時美元偏強 ，當通膨回落
與利率下降時美元偏弱。
17
